Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the logic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 550. The moduli space has dimension at most 646 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_550(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 550. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^646(S, F) is trivial. QED.
